1a1(sna tshogs): @#//shes phyin sna tshogs kyi bskor rnams bzhugs so//he/
1b1(sna tshogs): @#//rgyab bar skad do//
2a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /ar+ya pra dz+nya pa ra 
2a2(sna tshogs): mi ta sany+tsa ya ga thA/bod 
2b1(sna tshogs): skad du 'phags pa shes rab kyi pha rol 
2b2(sna tshogs): tu phyin pa bsdud pa tshigs su bcad pa/'phag 
2b3(sna tshogs): pa 'jam dpal la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas 
3a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /yang bcom ldan 'das kyis    
3a2(sna tshogs): 'khor bzhi po de dag yang dag par rab du dga' 
3a3(sna tshogs): bar mdzad pa'i phyir yang /shes rab kyi pha       
3b1(sna tshogs): rol tu phyin pa 'di ston cing /de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag       
3b2(sna tshogs): bka' stsal to//dga' dang gus dang dad pa'i mchog ni nyer 
3b3(sna tshogs): bzhag te/sgrib pa nyon mongs gsal nas dri ma las 'das   
3b4(sna tshogs): pa/'gro don mngon zhugs nges pa'i shes rab pha rol phyin/
4a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /gang la dpa' rnams spyod pa de ni mnyan par gyis//
4a2(sna tshogs): 'dzam bu gling 'dir chu klung ji snyed cig 'bab cing //me tog 'bras 
4a3(sna tshogs): ldan sman dang nags tshal bskyed byed pa//ma dros gnas pa'i klu 
4a4(sna tshogs): dbang klu bdag rten gnas te//de ni klu yi bdag po de yi mthu       
4b1(sna tshogs): dpal yin//rgyal ba'i nyan thos pa dag ji snyed chos ston dang //'chang dang rigs pa 
4b2(sna tshogs): dag dang ldan par brjod pa dang //mchog 'phags bde ba byed dang de yi 'bras thob pa//de 
4b3(sna tshogs): dag kun kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i skyes bu'i mthu//ci phyir zhe na rgyal bas chos tshul 
4b4(sna tshogs): gang bstan pa//de la mi mchog slob mar gyur pas mngon bslabs shing //mngon sum byas 
4b5(sna tshogs): nas bslab pa ji bzhin byed de//sangs rgyas mthus byed rang gi stobs kyi mthus ma yin/
5a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /gang la shes rab pha rol tu phyin mchog mi dmigs shing //byang chub sems      
5a2(sna tshogs): dpa' mi dmigs byang chub sems mi dmigs//de skad thos nas rmongs pa med cing mi skrag pa//byang chub 
5a3(sna tshogs): sems dpa' de ni bde gshegs shes rab spyod//gzugs med tshor ba med cing 'du shes sems dpa' 
5a4(sna tshogs): med//de la rnam shes gnas ni rdul tsam yod pa'ang min//de chos kun la mi gnas gnas pa med par 
5a5(sna tshogs): spyod//yongs su bzung med bde gshegs rnams kyi byang chub thob//ji ltar kun du rgyu ba bzod sbyangs shes
5b1(sna tshogs): pa yis//dmigs med phung po rnam par 'jig pa 'byung ba ltar//byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig de ltar chos shes pa//mya
5b2(sna tshogs): ngan las 'das la mi reg de ni shes rab gnas//yang 'di shes rab 'di ni gang yin su yi yin//gang las yin zhes chos
5b3(sna tshogs): 'di thams cad stong par rtog/nye bar rtags nas zhum pa med cing skrag med pa//byang chub sems dpa' de ni byang chub nye ba yin//
5b4(sna tshogs): gal te mi shes bzhin du gzugs su 'du shes shing //tshor ba sems pa rnam shes phung por spyod byed la//phung 'di stong zhes rtog
5b5(sna tshogs): na byang chub sems dpa' ni//mtshan ma la spyod skye med gnas la dad ma yin//gang zhig gzugs min tshor min 'du shes sems
5b6(sna tshogs): dpa' min//rnam par shes la mi spyod gnas pa med par spyod//de ni spyod ces bya bar mi dmigs shes rab brtan//skye med blo
6a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /ldan ting 'dzin zhi ba mchog la reg/byang chub sems gang de ltar 'dir bdag zhir gnas pa//de ni sngon gyi de 
6a2(sna tshogs): bzhin gshegs pas lung bstan yin//bdag ni mnyam par bzhag ce 'am langs zhes rloms sems med//ci phyir zhe na chos kyi rang 
6a3(sna tshogs): bzhin yongs shes phyir//de ltar spyod na     bde gshegs rnams kyi shes par spyod//des ni spyod med spyod pa yin par rab shes phyir//
6a4(sna tshogs): chos gang spyod pa de yang dmigs par mi 'gyur te//'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//gang zhig yod pa ma yin de ni med ces 
6a5(sna tshogs): bya//byis pa rnams kyis de rtags yod dang med par byed//yod dang med pa 'di gnyis med pa'i chos yin te//byang chub sems dpa' gang 
6a6(sna tshogs): gis 'di shes nges par 'byung//gang 'di phung po lnga dag sgyu ma 'drar shes shing //sgyu ma gzhan dang phung po gzhan du mi byed la//sna tshogs 
6b1(sna tshogs): 'du shes bral zhing nye bar zhi spyod pa//de ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//dge ba'i bshes dang ldan zhing lhag mthong ldan gyur pa//rgyal 
6b2(sna tshogs): ba rnams kyi yum thos skrag par yod mi 'gyur//gang zhig sdig pa'i grogs ldan gzhan gyi dring 'jog pa//de ni snod so ma btang chus reg zhig pa bzhin//
6b3(sna tshogs): ci phyir 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' bya zhe na//chags pa spyod byed kun la chags pa zad par 'dod//rgyal ba rnams kyi byang chub chags med gyur la reg/
6b4(sna tshogs): des na de ni byang chub sems dpa'i ming 'thob po//ci phyir 'di ni byang chub ses dpa' che zhe na//sems can tshogs rnams mang po'i mchog tu gyur pa dang //
6b5(sna tshogs): sems can khaMs kyi lha rnams chen po gcod pa ste//de phyir sems dpa' chen po zhes ni rab brjod do//gtong ba che dang blo che mthu che dang //rgyal ba rnams kyi
6b6(sna tshogs): theg chen mchog la zhug pa dang //go cha chen po bgos shing bdud kyi sgyu 'dul ba//de yi phyir na sems dpa' che zhes rab du brjod//dper na sgyu ma mkhan gyis
6b7(sna tshogs): gzhi mdor sprul byas te//skye bo phal chen mgo mang bye ba gcod byed pa'i//bsad bya de dag ci 'dra de ltar byang chub sems//'gro kun sprul 'drar rab shes de
7a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /la 'jigs pa med//gzugs dang 'du shes tshor ba dang ni sems dpa' dang //rnam par shes pa ma bcing+sa 
7a2(sna tshogs): ma grol yod pa min//de ltar byang chub la 'jug zhum pa'i sems med na//khang (khang ldi ni )ni gang zag dam pa rnams kyi go cha chog /ci phyir 'di ni byang chub theg chen bya zhe na/
7a3(sna tshogs): de gang zhon nas sems can thams (mya ngan 'a)cad ('o)zo//theg pa 'di ni namakha' 'dra gzhal med te/dga' skyid bde (ba)mngon par thob byed theg pa'i mchog/gang zhig zhon nas 
7a4(sna tshogs): phyogs su 'gro ba dmigs bu med mya ngan 'das par 'gro bar gsungs pa 'gro mi dmig/dper na mi shi de yis 'gro rgyu med pa bzhin/rgyu des ni mya ngan 'das zhes rab tu 
7a5(sna tshogs): brjod /byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mtha' dang phyi ma'i mtha' dang/da ltar byung ba'i dmigs dus gsum dag pa ste/gang dag de ni 'dus ma byas yin spros 
7a6(sna tshogs): med de/'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin/byang chub sems mkhas rig pa gang gi dus kyi tshe /skye med rnam par bsam te 'di ltar spyod byed cing/
7a7(sna tshogs): snying rje chen po bskyed kyang sems can 'du shes med/'di ni shes rab pha rolaphyin mchog spyod pa yin/gal te sems can 'du shes sdug bsngal 'du shes bskyed/
7b1(sna tshogs): 'gro ba rnams kyi don bya sdug bsngal spang snyam ste/bdag dang sems can yongs su rtog byang chub sems dpa' ste/'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin /
7b2(sna tshogs): bdag ci 'dra ba de 'drar sems can thams cad shes/sems can thams cad ci 'dra de 'drar chos kun shes/skye ba med dang skye ba gnyi gar mi rtog pa/'di ni 
7b3(sna tshogs): shes rab pha rol phyin mchogaspyod pa yin/'jig rtan dag na mos man ji snyed yongs brjod pa/kun la skye ba dang ni yang dag 'das spangs nas/ye shes 'chi med 
7b4(sna tshogs): dam pa de las gzhal med 'thob/de phyir 'di ni shes rab pha rol phyi ces bya/byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig dogs med de ltar spyod /shes rab ldan te 
7b5(sna tshogs): mnyam nyid gnas par shes par bya/chos rnams rang bzhin med par yongs su shes gyur pa/'di ni shes rab pha rolaphyin mchog spyod pa yin/gang zhig 
7b6(sna tshogs): gzugs la mi gnas tshor la mi gnas shing/gang zhig 'du shes mi gnas sems par mi gnas la/rnam shes gang mi gnas pa chos nyid gnas pa ste/'di ni 
7b7(sna tshogs): shes rab pha ro phyin mchog spyod pa yin/rtag dang mi rtag bde dang sdug bsngal sdug mi sdug/bdag dang bdag med de bzhin nyid dang de bzhin stong/'bras bu thob 
8a1(sna tshogs): dang dgra bcom sa la mi gnas shing /rang rgyal sa dang de bzhin sangs rgyas sar mi gnas/ji ltar 'dren pa 'dus ma byas 
8a2(sna tshogs): khams mi gnas shing /de bzhin 'dus ma byas la yang mi gnas gnas med spyod/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' gnas med gnas/gnas med gnas yin 'di ni rgyal 
8a3(sna tshogs): bas gnas par gsungs/gang dag bde gshegs nyan thos 'gyur bar bya snyam dang /rang sangs rgyas dang de bzhin chos rgyal 'gyur 'dod pa/bzod pa 'di la ma brten 
8a4(sna tshogs): thob par mi nus te/dper na pha rol tshu rol 'gro dgos mi mthong bzhin/chos 'chad pa dang nyan dang bshad bya gang yin dang /'bras bu thob dang rgyan rgyal de bzhin 
8a5(sna tshogs): 'jig rten mgon/mkhas pa gsal bas thob pa'i mya ngan 'das gang yin/de kun sgyu ma lta bur de bzhin gshegs pas bstan/gang zag bzhi po 'di dag de la mi 
8a6(sna tshogs): skrag te/rgyal sras bden la mkhas dang phyir mi ldog pa dang /dgra bcom dri ma gsal cing /so ma nyi spangs pa dang /bzhi pa dge ba'i bshes kyis yongs su zin pa'o//
8a7(sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' mkhas rig de ltar spyod pa ni/dgra bcom sa lam yin rkyen rtags sa la min/thams cad mkhyen nyid ched du sangs rgyas chos rjes slob//
8b1(sna tshogs): gang zhig slob la mi slob de slob yin/gzugs 'phel yongasu nyams dang/yongasu bzungs phyir min/chos rnams sna tshogs yongasu bzungs phyir mi slob par/slob 
8b2(sna tshogs): cing thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyang yongs 'dzin la/des 'byung gang yin 'di ni yon tan dga' ba'i slob/gzugs ni shes rab ma yin gzugs la shes rab med/
8b3(sna tshogs): rnam shes tshor dang sems dpa' 'di dag ni/shes rab mi yin 'di dag la yang shes rab med/'di ni namakha'i khams dang mtshungs te tha dad med/dmigs pa 
8b4(sna tshogs): rnams kyi rang bzhin de ni pha mtha' med/sems can rang bzhin gang yin de yang pha mtha' med/namakha'i khams kyi rang bzhin de yang pha mtha' med/'jig rten 
8b5(sna tshogs): mkhyen pa'i shes rab de yang pha mtha' med/'du shes tshu rol yin zhes 'dren pas rab tu sgrags/'du shes rnam par bshig nas spong ba pha rol 'gro//gang dag 
8b6(sna tshogs): 'du shes bral ba 'di ni rjes thob po//de dag pha rol phyin gnas ston pa'i bka' la gnas/gal te ston pas kyang ni gang ga'i bye snyed kyi/bskal par bzhugs te sems 
8b7(sna tshogs): can zhes sgra yongs sgrags kyang/bzod nas dag pas sems can skye bar ga la 'gyur/'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin/gang tshe nga ni pha rol phyin pa'i mchog 'di 
9a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /dang/'thun par smra gyur de tshe sngon gyis skyes mchog gis/ma 'ongs pa'i dus na sangs rgyas 'gyur ro//nga lung bstan par 
9a2(sna tshogs): gyur ces rgyal ba de skad gsungs/shes rab pha rol phyin pa gang la mgon spyod pa/'di na gang zhig gus byas 'dzin dang chub byed pa/de la dug dang mtshon (ma dang )dang chus mi 
9a3(sna tshogs): tshugs/bdud dang bdud kyi phyogs kyis glags kyang rnyed mi 'gyur/la las bde gshegs yongasu mya ngan 'das pa yi/mchod rten rin chen bdun las byas shing 
9a4(sna tshogs): mchod byas la/bde bar gshegs pa'i mchod rten de dag gang ga yi/bye ma snyed kyi bye ba phrag stong zhing bkang ste/yongasu brtag bzung zhing rnams bye ba mtha' yas 
9a5(sna tshogs): dpan/sems can ji snyed 'khod pa de dag thams cad kyis/las gzhan mi byed lha yi me tog spos rab dang/byug pas dus gsum bskal pa 'am/de bas lhag 
9a6(sna tshogs): mchod la/gang zhig gang las rnam 'dren stobs bcu 'byung 'gyur ba/bde bar gshegs kyi yum 'di glegs bam li bris te/'chang zhing me tog bdug pas 
9a7(sna tshogs): sti stang byed gyur na/mchod (brtan)bsod nams des ni char mi phod/rgyal ba'i shes rab pha rol phyin 'di rig sngags che /sems can khams mang mya ngan sdug bsngal 
9b1(sna tshogs): chos zhi byed/gang dag 'das dang gang dag phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten mgon//rig sngags 'di bslab+sa sman pa'i rgyal po bla med gyur/gang dag phan dang snying rtser 
9b2(sna tshogs): bces par spyad spyod pa/rig sngags 'di la bslabs nas mkhas pa byang chub reg/'dus byas bde dang 'dus ma byas de gang yin pa/bde ba de kun 'di las 
9b3(sna tshogs): 'byung bar rig par bya/sa bon btab nas sa la gnas te ('bras)'gyur ba/tshogs pa rnyed nas rnam mang gzugs dag rnam par skye/pha rol phyin lnga byang chub yon tan ji snyed pa/
9b4(sna tshogs): de dag thams cad shes rab pha rol phyin las skye/'khor lo bsgyur rgyal lam gang nas ni rtag 'gro ba/lam de nyid nas rin chen bdun dang dpung tshogs kun/gang 
9b5(sna tshogs): nas rgyal ba'i shes rab pha rol phyin 'di 'gro/de nyid nas ni     yon tan chos kun 'gro bar 'gyur/rgyal bas rgyal byin dri ba yongasi dras lan gsol ba/gal te 
9b6(sna tshogs): gang ga'i bye snyed sangs rgyas zhing gyur la/de kun rgyal ba'i ring srel byur bur bltams gyur kyang/bdag ni shes rab pha rol phyin pa 'di nyid len/ci slad ce na sku gdung 
9b7(sna tshogs): mi gus bdag ma lags/'on kyang shes rab yongasu sgos bas mchod par bgyi/dper na rgyal po rten mi rim 'gro thob pa ltar/sangs rgyas ring bsrel shes rab pha 
10a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /rol phyin la brten/nor bu rin chen yon tan kun ldan ring thang med/gab rtse gang du rtsal ba der ni phyag bgyi 'os/de phyung 
10a2(sna tshogs): gyur kyang gab rtse la ni dga' bgyid te /yon tan de dag nor bu rin chen de yi lags/de bzhin shes rab pha rol phyin mchog yon tan gyis/rgyal ba mya ngan 'das kyang ring bsrel mchod 
10a3(sna tshogs): pa bsnyes /de ltas gang zhig rgyal ba'i yon tan 'dzin 'tshal des/shes rab pha rol phyin blang 'di ni thar pa lags/sbyin pa byin ba'i sngon du 'gro ba shes rab ste/tshul khrims 
10a4(sna tshogs): bzod pa brtson 'grus bsam gtan de bzhin no//dge chos chud mi gsan phyir yongasu 'dzin pa yin/'di ni chos rnams thams cad tshul gcig ston par byed/dper na 'dzam 
10a5(sna tshogs): bu'i gling na shing rnams bye ba stong/rnam pa sna tshogs rnam pa du ma gzugs mang ba/grib mar gyur pa'i grangasu brjod ba ma gtogs par/grib ma sna tshogs med cing bye brag yod 
10a6(sna tshogs): ma yin/de bzhin rgyal ba'i pha rol phyin lnga 'di dag kyang/shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i ming nyid thob par 'gyur /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid phyir yongs su bsngos byas na/byang 
10a7(sna tshogs): chub ces byar drug po 'di kun ro cig 'gyur/byang chub sems dpa' gal te rab tu mi shes bzhin//gzugs dang 'du shes tshor dang sems dpa' rnam par shes//
10b1(sna tshogs): mi rtag yongs su ston pa chos la spyod pa ste/mkhas pa nam yang chos rnams 'jig par mi byed do//gang la gzugs min tshor min 'du shes mi dmigs shing //rnam par
10b2(sna tshogs): shes pa ma yin sems dpa' mi dmigs la/chos kun skye med stong pa'i tshul du rab shes pa/'di ni shes rab kyi pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin/la las gang ga'i klung gi bye snyed
10b3(sna tshogs): zhing rnams dang/sems can ji snyed 'khod kun dgra bcom nyid btul bas/gang gis shes rab pha rol phyin 'di yig bris te/sems can mchog la legs byin bsod nams
10b4(sna tshogs): khyad par 'phags/ci phyir zhe na smra mchog de dag 'dir bslabs nas/chos rnams mtha' dag stong pa nyid du 'dir ston byed/de thos nyan thos myur du rnam par grol
10b5(sna tshogs): bar 'gyur la reg/rang rgyal byang chub dang ni sangs rgyas byang chub reg/myug gu med par 'jig rten sdod 'byung yong pa med na/de la yal 'dab me tog 'bras bu
10b6(sna tshogs): ga la 'byung/byang chub sems med rgyal 'byung 'jig rten yong med na/'bras bu brgya byin tshangs pa nyan thos ga la 'byung /nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gang tshe 'od kyi dra ba
10b7(sna tshogs): 'gyed/de tshe sems can las byed brtson pa de bzhin du/mkhas pa shes phyir 'jig rten byang chub sems byung na/ye shes kyis ni sems can yon tan chos dang ldan/
11a1(sna tshogs): dper na ma dros gnas pa'i klu bdag med gyur na//'dzam bu'i gling 'dir chu rlung 'bab par ga la 'gyur//chu rlung med na 
11a2(sna tshogs): me tog 'bras bu 'byung mi 'gyur//rgya mtsho la yang rin chen gzugs mang med par 'gyur//de bzhin 'dir yang byang chub sems med bde gshegs kyi//ye shes 
11a3(sna tshogs): 'jig rten 'di dag kun tu ga la 'byung//ye shes med na yon tan 'phel med byang chub med//rgya mtsho 'dra ba'i sangs rgyas chos kyang med par 'gyur//'jig 
11a4(sna tshogs): rten 'di na snang byed srog chags ji snyed pa//kun gyis snang bar bya phyir 'od rnams byung ba bas//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'od zer gcig phyung ches mchog ste//snang byed 
11a5(sna tshogs): tshogs kyi 'od rnams kun gyis char mi phod//nyan thos tshogs rnams ji snyed sbyin dang tshul khrims dang//bsgom dang rab tu ldan pa'i bsod nams tshogs bskyed 
11a6(sna tshogs): pa 'ang//byang chub sems dpa'i rjes su yid rang sems gcig la//nyan thos tshogs kyi bsod nams phung pos char mi phod//'das dus sngon gyi sangs rgyas bye 
11a7(sna tshogs): ba khrag khrig gang //zhing mang mtha' yas bye stong dag na gang bzhugs dang//gang yang mya ngan 'das pa 'jig rten mgon po rnams//sdug bsngal zad par 
11b1(sna tshogs): bya phyir rin chen chos ston pa//dang por byang chub mcheg du sems bskyed bskyed pa nas//rnam par 'dren pa rnams kyi dmi chos zad kyi dus//de bar rgyal ba de 
11b2(sna tshogs): dag bsod nams gang yin dang//pha rol phyin dang ldan dang sangs rgyas chos gang dang//gang yang sangs rgyas sras rnams dang ni nyan thos dang//slob dang mi slob 
11b3(sna tshogs): zag bcas zag med pa//bsdus nas byang chub sems dpa' rjes su yi rang zhing//'gro ba'i don rgyu byang chub phyir ni thams cad bsngo//yongs bsngo gal te 
11b4(sna tshogs): sems su 'du shes 'byung 'gyur zhing/byang chub 'du shes yongs bsngo sems ni gsum la chags//dmigs pa yod pas yongs su bsngos par tshud ma 
11b5(sna tshogs): yin//gal te 'di ltar chos 'di 'gags shing zag pa dang//gang du yongs su bsngo ba de yang zad pa dang/chos kyis chos la nam yang mi sngo shes gyur na//de ltar 
11b6(sna tshogs): rab tu shes pas ra yongs su bsngos pa yin//gal te mtshan mar byed na de ni bsngo ma yin//ci te mtshan ma med na byang chub bsngo ma yin//ji ltar dug dang 'dres 
11b7(sna tshogs): pa'i kha zas bzad za ba//dkar po'i chos la dmigs pa 'ang de 'drar rgyal bas gsungs/de ltas 'di ltar yongasu bsngo ba bslab bya ste//ji ltar rgyal ba de dag dge 
12a1(sna tshogs): ba de mkhyen pa'i//rnam pa gang yin 'byung gang mtshan nyid gang yin la//rjes su yid rang de ltar de ltar yongs su bsngo//de 
12a2(sna tshogs): ltar bsod nams byang chub yongs su bsngo byed na//dug med sangs rgyas mi spong rgyal bas gsungs pa smra//de ltar yongs bsngo dpa' bo 'jig rten dmigs can gyi//byang 
12a3(sna tshogs): chub sems dpa' ji snyed thams cad zil gyis gnon//dmus long dmigs (bu) med pa bye ba khrag khrig rnams//lam yang mi shes grong khyer 'jug par ga la 'gyur//shes rab 
12a4(sna tshogs): med na mig med pha rol phyin lnga 'dis//dmigs bu med pas byang chub reg par nus ma yin//gang tshe shes rab kyis ni rab tu zin gyur pa//de tshe mig snyed gyur cing 'di yi 
12a5(sna tshogs): ming 'thob po//dper ni ro mo'i las ni yongs zin mig med pa//ji srid mig ma dris par rngan pa mi thob bzhin//gang tshe 'dus byas 'dus ma byas dang dkar nag chos/
12a6(sna tshogs): shes rab rnam par bshig na rdul tsam mi dmigs tshe//'jig rten dag na shes rab pha rol phyin grangs 'gro//nam mkha' gang la cung zad mi gnas de dang 'dra//sems 
12a7(sna tshogs): can//sdug bsngal reg pa khrag khrig mang grol sems//sems can 'du shes yongs rtog byang chub sems dpa' ste//'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//
12b1(sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig sngon chad spyod pa'i tshe//pha rol phyin 'dir spyad pa mkhas pa som nyi med//thos ma thag tu de ni ston pa'i 'du shes bskyed//des ni byang chub 
12b2(sna tshogs): zhi ba myur du rtogs par 'gyur//sngon spyod tshe na sangs rgyas khrag khrig bkur byas kyang //rgyal ba'i shes rab pha rol phyin la ma dad na//thos nas blo chung de yis 'di ni pong byed de//
12b3(sna tshogs): spangs nas skyabs med gyur pa de ni mnar med 'gro//de lta bas na gal te sangs rgyas ye shes mchog/reg par 'dod na rgyal ba'i yum 'dir dad par bgyis//dper na/
12b4(sna tshogs): tshong pa rin chen gling du song nas ni//zong zad slar log lta bur gyur na mi rung ngo //gzugs kyi dag pa 'bras bu dag par rig par bya//'bras bu gzugs dag thams cad mkhyen 
12b5(sna tshogs): nyid dag par 'gyur//thams cad mkhyen nyid 'bras bu dag dang gzugs dag pa//nam mkha'i khams dang mtshungs te dbyer med spyad tu med//dpa' bo gar spyod shes rab pha rol 
12b6(sna tshogs): phyin pa yis//khams gsum yang dag 'das las rnam par grol ba'ang min//nyon mongs gsal bar gyur kyang skye ba ston par byed//rga dang na dang 'chi ba med kyang 'chi 
12b7(sna tshogs): 'pho ston//'gro ba 'di dag ming dang gzugs kyi 'dam la chags//rlung gi 'khor lo lta bu'i 'khor ba'i 'khor lor 'khyam//'gro ba 'khrul pu ri dgas 
13a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /rgyar chud 'drar shes nas//shes rab ldan pa mkha' la bya bzhin rnam par rgyu //spyod pa yongs dag gang zhig gzugs
13a2(sna tshogs): la mi spyod cing//rnam shes 'du shes tshor ba sems dpar mi spyod na//de ltar spyod pa chags pa thams cad yongs spong zhing//chags las rnam grol bde gshegs 
13a3(sna tshogs): rnams kyi shes rab spyod//de ltar spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' mkhas gsal ba//chags pa spyad nas 'gro la chags pa med par 'gro//nyi ma sgra can bza' bral 
13a4(sna tshogs): lha der 'dug pa dang//me btang rtsa dang shing dang nags tshal sreg pa bzhin//chos rnams thams cad rang bzhin dag cing yongs dag par//byang chub sems dpa' 
13a5(sna tshogs): shes rab pha rol phyin lta na/byed pa po yang mi dmigs chos kun mi dmigs te//'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//rgyal ba la ni lha rgyal brgya byin gyi 
13a6(sna tshogs): zhus pa//byang chub sems dpa' shes rab pa spyod pa ji ltar brtson//phung po khams la rdul tsam brtson par mi byed cing//phung por mi brtson de ni byang chub sems 
13a7(sna tshogs): dpa'i brtson//gang zhig chos 'di sprul dang sgyu ma 'drar thos nas//som nyi med cing slob la phyir zhing sbyor byed pa//sems can de ni ring nas zhugs rig bya//sangs 
13b1(sna tshogs): rgyas bye ba khrag khrig mang la lhag byas yin//dgon pa'i lam ni dpag tshad mang por zhugs pa'i mis//gnag rdzi 'tshams kyi nags tshal phun sum tshogs mthong na//
13b2(sna tshogs): 'di dag grong dang khyer nye ba'i rtags snyam te//dbugs phyin rnyed par gyur cing chom rkun 'jig pa med//de dag byang chub yongs tshol tshe rgyal rnams kyis ni//shes rab pha rol
13b3(sna tshogs): phyin 'di mnyan pa gang thob pa//dbugs phyin rnyed paragyur cing chom rkun 'jigs pa med//dgra bcom sa la ma yin rkyen rtogs sa la min//mi zhig rgya mtsho'i chu ni lta
13b4(sna tshogs): phyir 'gro ba yis//gal te shing dang nags tshal ri mthong da dung ring//ci ste rtags ma mthong na rgya mtsho chen po ni//nye bar gyur to snyam ste de la som nyi med//de ltar byang chub
13b5(sna tshogs): dam pa rab zhugs rgyal ba yi//shes rab pha rol phyin pa 'di nyan rig par bya//rnam par 'dren pas mngon sum lung bstan ma gyur kyang//sangs rgyas byang chub ring
13b6(sna tshogs): por mi thogs bdag reg 'gyur//dpyid dus bzang la ldong po lo ma lhags gyur na//yal gas ring po mi thogs lo 'bras me teg skrun//shes rab pha rol phyina'adi
13b7(sna tshogs): su yi lag thob pa//ring por mi thogs 'dren pa rnams kyi byang chub 'thob//dper na bud med sgrum gang sdug bsngal nyen gyur na//de ni de yi rtsa ba'i dus la bab zhe bya//
14a1(sna tshogs): de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' rgyal ba'i shes rab nyan//dga' dang 'dun pa bskyed pa myur du byang chub reg/shes rab 
14a2(sna tshogs): pha rol phyin mchog spyod tshe rnal 'byor pa//gzugs kyi 'phel ba dang ni yongs su 'grib mi mthong //gang zhig chos dang chos min chos dbyings mi mthong zhing //mya 
14a3(sna tshogs): ngan 'das la mi reg de ni shes rab gnas//gang zhig 'dir spyod sangs rgyas chos rnams mi rtog ste//stobs dang rdzu 'phrul rkang pa byang chub zhi mi rtogs//rnam par 
14a4(sna tshogs): mi rtog rtog bral byin gyis brlabs kyis spyod//'di ni shes rab pha rol phyin mchog spyod pa yin//smra ba'i zla ba sangs rgyas la ni rab 'byor zhus//yon tan dga' ba 
14a5(sna tshogs): rnams kyi bar du gcod gang lags//ston pas bka' bstsal bar du gcod par 'gyur mang ste/de las re zhig cung zad tsam zhig yongs su brjod//rgyal ba'i shes 
14a6(sna tshogs): rab pha rol phyin 'di yig 'bri che//spobs pa tha dad rnam pa du ma 'byung 'gyur zhing //'gro ba'i don ma byas par glog bzhin myur du yang //yongs su ma nyams 
14a7(sna tshogs): par 'gyur ba 'di ni bdud kyi las//'di bshad tshe na kha cig som nyi za 'gyur te//'dren pas 'dir ni bdag gi ming yang yongs ma brjod//rigs dang sa dang rus kyang yongs 
14b1(sna tshogs): su ma brjod cing //de ni mnyam par mi 'gyur spong 'gyur bdud kyi las//de ltar mi shes de dag rtsa ba bor nas ni//rmongs pas yal ga dag dang 'dab ma tshol bar 
14b2(sna tshogs): 'gyur//glang po rnyed kyang glang po'i rjes ni tshol ba ltar//shes rab pha rol phyin thos mdo tshol de bzhin no//dper na la las ro brgya ldan pa'i 
14b3(sna tshogs): zad rnyed nas//zad mchog rnyed nas zas ngan tshol bar byed pa ltar//byang chub sems dpa' sha pha rol phyin pa 'di snyed nas//dgra bcom pas yis byang chub 
14b4(sna tshogs): tshol byed de bzhin no//sti stang 'dod par 'gyur zhing rnyed pa 'dod 'gyur la//lta dang bcas pa'i sems kyis khyim 'dris byed ldan zhing //chos btang nas ni chos min 
14b5(sna tshogs): bya ba spyod 'gyur ba//lam bor lam gol 'gro ba 'di ni bdud kyi las//gang gi tshe na 'dun cing dad gyur nas//chos kyi dam pa 'di ni nyan du song ba lags//de dag 
14b6(sna tshogs): gis ni chos smra bya bar ldan rig nas//dga' ba med cing yid mi bde bar gyur nas 'gro//de'i tshe na bdud kyis las 'byung bar 'gyur//de tshe gang dag gis ni dge slong mang 
14b7(sna tshogs): dkrugs nas//shes rab pha rol phyin 'di 'dzin pa mi 'gyur ba'i//bar du gcod pa rnam mang gzhan yang 'byung bar 'gyur//rin chen rin thang med pa rnyed gyur 
15a1(sna tshogs): gang dag yin//de dag dkon la dus rnams rtag tu 'tshe ba'ang mang//de bzhin rgyal ba'i shes rab pha rol 
15a2(sna tshogs): phyin pa mchog//rin chen chos kyang dkon la rtag tu 'tshe ba'ang mang //theg pa gsar du zhugs pa'i sems can blo chung ba//gang zhig dkon pa'i rin chen 'di ni ma 
15a3(sna tshogs): rnyed pa//de la bar chad bya phyir bdud ni spro bar 'gyur//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas de dag yongs su 'dzin la sbyor//mi zhig na bar gyur la bu ni mang yod pa//de kun yid 
15a4(sna tshogs): mi bde zhing de la rim 'gro byed//de bzhin phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams kyi sangs rgyas kyang//yum 'gyur shes rab dam pa 'di la dgongs pa mdzad//gang dag 'das 
15a5(sna tshogs): dang phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten mgon gang dang//ma 'ongs dus na 'byung 'gyur de dag 'di las 'byung//'jig rten ston pa rgyal ba rnams kyi bskyed byed yum//sems can gzhan 
15a6(sna tshogs): gyi sems kyi spyod pa ston par byed//'jig rten de bzhin nyid dang dgra bcom de bzhin nyid//rang rgyal de bzhin nyid dang rgyal sras de bzhin nyid//cig pa nyid de dngos bral 
15a7(sna tshogs): gzhan min de bzhin nyid//shes rab pha rol phyin pa de bzhin gshegs pas mkhyen//mkhas pas 'jig rten gnas sam mya ngan 'das kyang rung//chos nyid skyon med 
15b1(sna tshogs): chos rnams stong pa 'di gnas te//byang cub sems dpa' de bzhin nyid 'di rjes su rtogs//de phyir de bzhin gshegs zhes sangs rgyas mtshan gsol to//shes rab 
15b2(sna tshogs): pha rol phyin pa dga' tshal la rten pa//stob bcu rnam 'dren rnams kyi spyod yul 'di yin te//sems can sdug bsngal ngan song gsum las yongs 'don kyang //
15b3(sna tshogs): de dag nams kyang sems can 'gyur ba med//dper na seng ge ri sul rten nas 'jig med par//ri dgas phra mo mang skrag byed cing sgra sgrog ltar//mi'i seng ge shes 
15b4(sna tshogs): rab pha rol phyin brten nas//mu stegs can mang skrag byed 'jig rten sgra yang sgrogs//dper na nam mkhar gnas pa'i nyi ma'i 'od zer na//sa 'di skems par byed cing gzugs 
15b5(sna tshogs): kyang rab tu ston//de bzhin chos rgyal shes rab pha rol phyin rten pa//srid pa'i chu klung skems shing chos kyang rab tu ston//gzugs rnams mi mthong tshor ba dag 
15b6(sna tshogs): kyang mi mthong zhing //'du shes 'thong ba med la sems pa mi mthong zhing //gang la rnam par shes dang sems yid mthong med pa//'di ni chos mthong ba yang de bzhin 
15b7(sna tshogs): gshegs pas bstan//nam mkha' mthong zhes sems can tshig tu rab brjod pa//nam mkha' ji ltar mthong zhes don 'di rtag par gyis//de ltar chos mthong ba 
16a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /yang de bzhin gshegs pas bstan//mthong ba dpe gzhan gyis ni snyed par nus ma yin//gang gis de 
16a2(sna tshogs): ltar mthong ba de yis chos kun mthong//rgyal po btang snyoms 'dug cing blon po kun byed ltar//sangs rgyas mdzad dang nyan thos chos rnams ji snyed pa//de dag tham+sa
16a3(sna tshogs): cad shes rab pha rol phyin pas byed//rgyal po grong du mi 'gro yul 'khor mi 'gro bar//rang gi yul nas 'du ba thams cad sdud pa ltar//byang chub sems dpa' chos 
16a4(sna tshogs): nyid gang du 'ang mi 'gro la//sangs rgyas sa yi yon tan gang yin thams cad sdud//gang la bde gshegs byang chub sems dpa' dad bstan yod//shes rab pha rol 
16a5(sna tshogs): phyin pa mchog la bsam sbyor ba//nyan thos rang rgyal sa gnyis shin tu 'dus byas nas//zil gyi mi gnon rgyal ba'i byang chub myur du 'thob//dper na rgya mtshor 'jug 
16a6(sna tshogs): pa'i gru ni chag gyur la//mi gang ro 'am rtsa 'am shing ni mi 'dzin pa//chu dbus 'jig par 'gyur te dog+sa su phyin mi 'gyur //gang zhig 'dzin pa rgya mtsho'i pha rol phyin par 
16a7(sna tshogs): 'gyur//de ltar dad ldan dang ba thob pa gang zhig ni//shes rab pha rol phyin yum rjes su mi 'jug min//de dag thabs kyi yon tan shes rab yongs zin na//mchog 
16b1(sna tshogs): tu rmad byung bde gshegs byang chub myur du reg//dper na la las rdza so ma btang chu bskyed na//nyams chung phyir ni myur du 'jig par rig par bya//so btang bum par chu bskyed 
16b2(sna tshogs): lam du 'jig pa yi//'jigs pa med cing bde bar khyim du phyin par 'gyur//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' dang pa mang 'gyur kyang//shes rab rnam      par nyams na 
16b3(sna tshogs): myur du ma rungs 'gyur//dad pas shes rab de ni yongasu zin gyur na//sa gnyis shin tu 'das te byang chub mchog thob 'gyur//dper na bcos legs ma byas gru ni rgya      
16b4(sna tshogs): mtsho'i nang//nor dang bcas shing tshor par bcas te 'jig par 'gyur//gru de legs par bcos legs byas dang ldan gyur na//mi 'jig nor dang bcas pa ngogs su phyin 
16b5(sna tshogs): par 'gyur //de ltar byang chub sems dpa' dad pas yongs bsgom kyang//shes rab med na byang chub las ni myur nyams 'gyur//de nyid pha rol phyin pa'i mchog ldan 
16b6(sna tshogs): na//ma smas ma nyams rgyal ba'i byang chub reg par 'gyur //lo brgya nyi shu lon te rgas shing sdug bsngal mi//langs kyang bdag nyid 'gro bar nus pa ma yin te/
16b7(sna tshogs): de ni g.yas g.yon gnyis nas mi yis brten byas nas//'gyel ba'i 'jig pa med cing bde glag 'gro bar 'gyur//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' shes rab stob 
17a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /chung ba //rab tu zhugs par gyur kyang bar du nyams par 'gyur //de nyid thabs dang shes rab mchog gis yongs 
17a2(sna tshogs): zin na//mi nyams de bzhin gshegs kyi byang chub reg par 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa' dang po'i las kyi sa gnas gang//lhag pa'i bsam pas sangs rgyas mchog 
17a3(sna tshogs): zhugs pa//slob ma bzang po bla ma gus ldan de dag gis//bla ma mkhas pa rnams la rtag tu brten par bya//ci phyir zhe na mkhas pa'i yon tan de las 'byung//de dag shes 
17a4(sna tshogs): rab pha rol phyin pa rjes su ston//sangs rgyas chos rnams dge ba'i bshes la brten to zhes //yon tan kun gyi mchog mnga' rgyal bas de skad gsungs//sbyin dang tshul 
17a5(sna tshogs): khrims bzod dang de bzhin brtson 'grus dang//bsam gtan shes rab byang chub yongs su bsngo bar gyis//byang chub phung por zhen nas mchog 'dzin ma byed ces //dang 
17a6(sna tshogs): po'i las (can )dang (de ltar )skad bstan par bya//de ltar spyod pa legs mcho smra ba'i zla ba rnams//'gro ba'i skyabs dang dpung gnyen dang ni gnas yin te//brten dang blo dang gling dang yong+sa 
17a7(sna tshogs): 'dren ('don 'dod pa//gsal byed sgron ma chos mchog smra ba mi 'khrugs yin//grags chen ldan rnams bya dga' go cha gyon byed te//phung po khams dang skye mchad rnams kyi 
17b1(sna tshogs): go cha min/theg pa gsum 'du shes bral zhing yongs su bzung ba med/mi ldog g.yo ba med cing mi 'khrugs chos can yin/de dag de 'dra'i chos dang ldan zhing spros pa 
17b2(sna tshogs): med/the tshom som nyi yid gnyis bral zhing don dang ldan/shes rab pha rol phyin pa thos nas mi nur ba//gzhan gyi dri nga mi 'jog cing mi ldog rig par bya//'dren 
17b3(sna tshogs): pa rnams kyi chos 'di zab ste mthong ba dga'//sus kyang rtogs pa med cing thob 'gyur med pa ltar//de phyir phan mdzad rtser ldan byang chub rnyed nas 'di//sems can 
17b4(sna tshogs): tshogs sus shes snyem thugs khral chung bar mdzad//sems can rnams ni gnas la dga' zhing yul rnams 'dod//'dzin la gnas shing mi mkhas blun rmongs mun 
17b5(sna tshogs): ba bzhin//thob par bya ba'i chos ni gnas med 'dzin med te//des na 'jig rten dag dang brtsod pa 'byung ba yin//nam mkha'i khams ni shar phyogs dang lho phyogs dang /
17b6(sna tshogs): de bzhin nub kyi phyogs dang byang phyogs pha mtha' yas//steng dang 'og dang phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten rnams na 'ang yod//tha dad gyur pa med cing bye brag gyur pa med/
17b7(sna tshogs): 'das pa'i de bzhin nyid gang ma byon de bzhin nyid//da ltar de bzhin nyid gang dgra bcom de bzhin nyid//chos kun de bzhin nyid gang rgyal ba'i de bzhin nyid//
18a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /chos kyi de bzhin nyid 'di thams cad bye brag med//bde gshegs byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig thob 'dod 
18a2(sna tshogs): pa//thabs dang ldan pas shes rab pha rol phyin la sbyor //'dren pa'i shes rab med na thob par 'gyur ma yin//bya zhig lus che dpag tshad brgya rtsa lnga bcu par//gyur 
18a3(sna tshogs): la 'dab bshog zad de nyams rtsal yod min na//de yis sum bcu rtsa gsum gnas nas 'dzam gling 'dir//bdag nyid 'chongs (ma)der ni nyams nyes gyur ba bzhin//rgyal 
18a4(sna tshogs): ba rnams kyi pha rol phyin pa lnga 'di yang //bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig mang por sgrub byas shing//smon lam mtha' yas rgya chen 'jig rten kyang //thabs med shes rab bral 
18a5(sna tshogs): bas nyan thos nyid du ltung//sangs rgyas theg pa 'di la des par 'byung 'dod gang//'gro ba kun la sems snyoms pa ma'i 'du shes dang//phan pa'i sems dang byams 
18a6(sna tshogs): pa'i yid kyis brtul bya zhing//tha ba med cing drang la tshig 'jam smra bar bya//'jig rten mgon la gnas brtan rab 'byor yongs zhus pa//yon tan (ba)rgya mtsho'i nyon 
18a7(sna tshogs): mongs ma mchis rtag brtan gsol//mthu che ba dang ji ltar slar ldog mi 'gyur ba//de ltar yon tan phyogs tsam rgyal bas lung bstan gsol//tha dang 'du shes 
18b1(sna tshogs): bral zhing rigs pa ldan tshig spra//dge sbyong dag dang bram ze gzhan la rten ma byed//mkhas pa dus rnams rtag tu ngan song gsum po spangs//de dag dge bcu'i las kyi 
18b2(sna tshogs): lam la mngon par brtson//zang zing med par 'gro la chos ni rjes su ston//chos la cig tu dga' zhing rtag tu tshig 'jam ldan//'gro 'chag nyal dang 'dug par shes bzhin 
18b3(sna tshogs): rab tu ldan//gnya' shing gang tsam lta zhing 'gro la sems 'khrul med//gnang byas gtsang ma'i gos 'chang gsum po dben zhing dag//rnyed 'dod ma yin khyu mchog rtag 
18b4(sna tshogs): tu chos 'dod pa//bdud kyi yul las 'das shing gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog/bsam gtan bzhi la bsam gtan bsam gtan der mi gnas//grags pa 'dod pa yin khro 
18b5(sna tshogs): bas sems dkris med//khyim par gyur kyang rtag tu dngos po kun ma chags//'tsho ba'i phyir ni ya ngas longs spyod tshol mi byed//drag shul sbyor sngags mi byed bud med kyi 
18b6(sna tshogs): sbyor sngags min//'dod pa'i las can pho mor 'gyur zhes ston mi byed//rab tu dben pa'i shes rab pha rol phyin mchog brtson//thabs dang rtsod dang bral zhing byams pa'i 
18b7(sna tshogs): sems kyang brtan//kun mkhyen 'dod cing bstan la rtag tu gzhol sems ldan//mtha' 'khob kla klo skye ba'i yul mtha' rnam par spangs//rang gi sa la dogs med rtag 
19a1(sna tshogs): tu ri ba 'dra//chos phyir srog kyang 'dor zhing rnam 'byor rab tu brtson//mi ldog rnams ni 'di dag yin par shes par bya//gzugs 
19a2(sna tshogs): dang tshor ba 'du shes sems pa rnam shes rnams//zab ste rang bzhin mtshan ma med cing rab zhi ba//dper na mda' yis rgya mtsho'i gting thug tshol ba ltar//shes rab kyis 
19a3(sna tshogs): ni yongs rtags phung po'i gting mi rnyed//byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig de ltar zab gyur cing //byed pa don dam chags med gang la phung po dang //khams dang skye mched 
19a4(sna tshogs): yod min chos 'di rtogs pa de'i//bsod nams yang dag 'grub pa de las ci zhig yod//dper na 'dod chags chos las spyod pa'i skyes bu zhig/bud med dus btab 
19a5(sna tshogs): de dang ma phrad de yi ni//nyin cig sems kyi rnam rtog spyod pa ji snyed pa//de snyed bskal pa'i byang chub sems dpas thob par 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa'-i gang zhig 
19a6(sna tshogs): bskal mang byed stong du//dgra bcom rang brgyal sbyin byed tshul khrims bsrung byed kyang //gang zhig shes rab pha rol phyin mchog ldan pa'i chos//brjod pa'i legs pa 
19a7(sna tshogs): sbyin dang tshul khrims char mi phod//byang chub sems dpa'i gang zhig shes rab mchog sgoms te//de las langs nas gos pa med pa'i chos spyod cing //de yang 'gro ba'i 
19b1(sna tshogs): don du byang chub rgyur bsngo na//dge ba de dang mnyam pa 'jig rten gsum na med//bsod nams de dang gsog dang de bzhin stong pa dang//gsob dang ya ma rla dang snying po med 
19b2(sna tshogs): shes pa//de ltar bde gshegs shes rab spyad spyod na//spyod tshe bsod nams dpag tu med pa yongs su 'dzin//sangs rgyas kyis ('bad)rab tu sbyar cing rab bstan pa'i//chos 
19b3(sna tshogs): 'di thams cad brjod pa tsam du rab shes na//bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig mang por brjod byas kyang//chos kyi dbyings la zad pa med cing 'phel ba med//rgyal ba rnam kyi pha rol 
19b4(sna tshogs): phyin ces bya ba gang //chos de rnams kyang ming tsam du ni yongs su bsgrags//yongs bsngo rlom sems med pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni//nyams pa med cing sangs rgyas 
19b5(sna tshogs): byang chub mchog reg 'gyur//'bru mar snying po 'bar ba phrad pa dang po yis//snying po tshigs (               )min de med pa yang de mi tshig//me lce phrad pa tha mas snying po tshig min la//me lce 
19b6(sna tshogs): tha ma med par snying po tshig min ltar//dang po'i sems kyis byang chub mchog reg ma yin la//de med par yang de la reg par nus ma yin//tha ma'i sems kyis byang chub zhi ba 
19b7(sna tshogs): thob min la//de med par yang do thob nus pa ma yin no//sa bon las ni myu gu me tog 'bras bu 'byung//de yang 'gags la shing yang med pa de min ltar//de bzhi dang bo'i 
20a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /sems kyang byang chub rgyu yin te//de yang 'gags blang chub med pa de ma yin//chu'i thigs pas bum pa dang po tha mi'i bar//rim 
20a2(sna tshogs): gyis nyung du de yongs 'gengs pa ltar//de bzhin dang po'i sems kyang byang chub mchog rgyu ste//rim gyis dkar po'i yon tan rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'gyur//stong pa mtshan ma 
20a3(sna tshogs): med par chos spyod cing//mya ngan 'das la mi reg mar mi spyod ba//dper na mnyam pa mkhas pa phar 'gro tshur 'gro yang//mtha' gnyis mi gnas rgya mtshor gnas pa med pa 
20a4(sna tshogs): bzhin//de ltar spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam bdag//stobs bcus lung bstan byang chub reg/'gyur rlom sems med//'di la ci yang med ces byang chub

20a6(sna tshogs): gyon te//phyi ma'i mu mthar rtag tu brtson zhing rab shes nas//yongs su skyo ba'i blo ni rdul tsam bskyed mi byed//byang chub sems dpa' rgyal ba'i shes rab sbyin byed cing/
20a7(sna tshogs): pung po 'di dag gdod nas stong zhing skye med shes//mnyam par ma gzhag sems can khams la snying rjer 'jug //bar skabs der yang sangs rgyas bcom (ldan )chos la yongs/
20b1(sna tshogs): mi mnyams//dper na skyes bu mkhas pa yon tan kun ldan zhing//stobs ldan mi thub brtson byas sgyu rtsal chog shes pa//'phongs dang bzo gnas mang po'i pha rol 
20b2(sna tshogs): phyin gyur cing//sgyu ma grub shes 'gro don rab tu 'dod pa zhig//pha dang ma dang chung mar bcas pa yongs bsdus nas//dgra byed mang po dgon pa'i lam du phyin pa dang//
20b3(sna tshogs): de'i dpa' zhing brtul phod skyes bu pha rol phyin gyur cing//de'i dpa' zhing rtul phod skyes bu mang sprul te//bde bar song zhing phyir yang khyim du 'ong ba ltar//de bzhin de tshe byang 
20b4(sna tshogs): chub sems dpa' mkhas pa yang//sems can kham+sa kun tu snying rje chen po yis//bdud bzhi dag dang sa gnyis las kyang rab 'das nas//ting 'dzin mchog la 
20b5(sna tshogs): gnas shing byang chub reg mi byed//mkha' la rlung rten de la chu yi phung po rten//de la sa chen 'di rten de la 'gro ba rten//sems can las la spyod pa'i rgyu ni de 'dra ste//
20b6(sna tshogs): namakha' gang la gnas yin don 'di bsam par gyis//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' stong pa nyid gnas pa/'gro la sems can shes pa'i smon lam brtan can gyi/
20b7(sna tshogs): bya ba rnam pa sna tshogs ston byed cing//mya ngan 'das la mi reg stong par gnas shing zhi//gang gi tshe na byang chub sems dpa' mkhas gsal ba//stong pa zhi ba'i ting 
21a1(sna tshogs): 'dzin dam pa 'di la spyod pa//rab skabs der ni mtshan ma rab tu bsgom mi byed//mtshan ma med par gnas 
21a2(sna tshogs): shing zhi la rab zhi spyod//bar snang phur ba'i bya la gnas yod ma yin ste//de la gnas pa ma yin sa la ltung mi 'gyur//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' rnam thar sgor 
21a3(sna tshogs): spyod pa//mya ngan 'das la mi reg mtshan mar spyod ma yin//dper na 'phongs brlabs so skyes bus gnam du mda' 'phangs te//mda' gzhan dag ni phyi zhin rgyud mar yongs 
21a4(sna tshogs): btags nas//snga ma'i mda' de sa la ltung ba'i skabs mi byed//mi de 'dod na mda' des la ltung bar 'gyur//de ltar shes rab pha rol phyin pa mchog spyod pa//shes 
21a5(sna tshogs): rab thabs stobs rdzu 'phrul gyis ni rnam spyod pa//de dag dge ba'i rtsa ba ji srid ma rtogs pa//de srid stong nyid dam pa thob mi byed//dper na dge slong rdzu 'phrul stobs 
21a6(sna tshogs): mchog ldan pa zhig/nam mkha'i gnas te gcig char chos 'phrul rnam par 'phrul//'gro dang 'chag pa dang ni nyal dang 'dug pa ston//de ni skyo ba med cing de la dub pa med//
21a7(sna tshogs): de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' mkhas pa ston gnas shing //ye shes rdzu 'phrul pha rol son la gnas pa med//'gro ba dag la bya ba rnam pa mtha' yas ston//
21b1(sna tshogs): skal pa bye bar skyo ba med cing dub pa med//dper na mi 'ga' g.yang sa chen por 'dug byed cing //lag pa gnyis gas gdugs gnyis 'dzin cing nam mkha' la//'chor 
21b2(sna tshogs): bar byed cing g.yang sa chen por lus bor nas//ltung bar mi 'gyur ji srid der ni 'gro bar 'gyur//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' mkhas pa snying rjer gnas//
21b3(sna tshogs): thabs dang shes rab gnyis kyi gdugs ni yongs bzung ste//chos rnams stong pa mtshan med smon pa med rtog cing //mya ngan 'das la reg pa med la chos kyang 
21b4(sna tshogs): mthong //dper na mi zhig rin chen gling song ste//rin chen rnyed nas phyir yang khyim du 'ong 'gyur pa//der ni ded dpon 'ba' zhig bde bar 'tsho byed cing //gnyen 
21b5(sna tshogs): gshes tshogs yid mi bder 'jog pa min pa ltar//de bzhin byang chub ses dpa' stong nyid rin chen gyi//gling du song ste bsam gtan dbang po stobs thob pa//
21b6(sna tshogs): mya ngan 'das la reg pa 'ba' zhig mngon dga' zhing //sems can sdug bsngal yid tu 'jog par 'gyur ma yin//dper na don gnyer tshong pa rgyus shes bya ba'i phyir//
21b7(sna tshogs): bar gyi grong khyer grong rdal grong gang yin par 'gro//der yang mi gnas rin chen glong du'ang mi gnas la//rig pa khyim du mi gnas lam la mkhas par 'gyur//
22a1(sna tshogs): de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' gsal te nyan thos dang //rang rgyal rnam kyi shes pa rnam grol kun la mkhas//
22a2(sna tshogs): de la mi gnas sangs rgyas ye shes gnas ma yin//'dus ma byas la mi gnas lam gyi tshul shes min//gang tshe 'gro la byams pa rjes su 'brel byas nas//ting 
22a3(sna tshogs): 'dzin stong pa mtshan med smon pa med spyod pa//de ni mya ngan 'das pa thob par 'gyur zhes 'am//'dus byed yin par gdags nus de ni gnas med do//dper na sprul 
22a4(sna tshogs): pa'i mi lus mi snang ma yin zhing //de ni ming gis kyang ni gdags par nus pa ltar//de bzhin byang chub sems dba' rnam thar sgo spyod pa//de ni ming gis kyang na 
22a5(sna tshogs): gdags par nus pa yin//gal te spyod pa dang ni dbang po yongs dris la//byang chub sems dpa' stong pa mtshan ma med pa'i chos//yongs su mi ston phyir 
22a6(sna tshogs): mi ltos pa sa yi chos//mi 'chad de ni lung ma bstan pa yin rig bya//dgra bcom sa dang rkyen gyi sangs rgyas ye shes dang //khams gsum dag la rmi lam 
22a7(sna tshogs): na yang mi 'dod cing /sangs rgyas rnams mthong 'gro la chos kyang ston byed pa//de ni mi ldog lung bstan yin par rig par bya//rmi lam ni sems can 
22b1(sna tshogs): ngan song gsum gnas mthong //skad cig de la ngan song rgyun gcod smon lam 'debs//bden pa'i byin gyis brlabs kyis med yi phung po zhi//de ni mi ldog lung 
22b2(sna tshogs): bstan yin par rig par bya//mi'i 'jig rten 'byung po'i gdon dang nad mang ba//phan dang snying brtser ldan pa'i bden pa'i byin gyis zhe//'on kyang rlom 
22b3(sna tshogs): sems med cing nga rgyal skyed med pa//de ni mi ldog lung bstan yin par rig par ba//ci ste de ni bden pa'i byin brlabs sna tshogs dang //'byor pas bdag lung 
22b4(sna tshogs): bstan snyam rlom sems 'byung ba 'am//gal te byang chub sems dpa' gzhan gyis lung bstan brlom//rlom sems gnas pa de ni blo chung shes par bya//
22b5(sna tshogs): ming gi gzhi las bdud ni nye bar 'ongs gyur nas//'di skad smras te 'di ni khyod dang pha ma dang //khyod kyis bdun mes rgyud kyi bar gyi ming yin zhing //gang tshe 
22b6(sna tshogs): khyod ni sangs rgyas 'gyur ba'i ming 'di yin//sbyangs sdom rnal 'byor ldan pa ci 'dra 'byung 'gyur la//khyod sngon yon tan tshul yang 'dra'o zhes brjod de//de 
22b7(sna tshogs): skad gang thos rlom sems byang chub sems dpa' ni//bdud kyis yongs su slang shing blo chung rig par bya//rab tu dben pa'i grong dang grong khyer ri sul dang //
23a1(sna tshogs): dgon pa dben pa'i gnas khrod rab tu rten byed cing //bdag bstod gzhan la smod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni//

23a3(sna tshogs): dgra bcom rang rgyal 'dod pa gzhan mi bskyed//'di ni bde bar gshegs pa'i sras kyis dben par gsungs//gang zhig dpag tshad lnga brgya yod pa'i ri'i sul//sbrul 
23a4(sna tshogs): gyis gang bar lo mang bye bar gnas byed kyang //dben pa 'di mi shes pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i de//lhag pa'i nga rgyal rnyed nas 'dre bar gnas pa yin//byang chub 
23a5(sna tshogs): sems dpa' 'gro ba'i don brtson bsam gtan dang //stobs dang dbang po rnam thar ting 'dzin thob de la//'di ni dgon dben spyod pa min snyam brnyas byed pa//de ni 
23a6(sna tshogs): bdud kyis spyod yul gnas zhes rgyal bas gsungs//gang zhig grong ngam 'on te dgon par gnas kyang rung //theg pa gnyis kyi sems bral byang chub mchog nges na/
23a7(sna tshogs): 'di ni 'gro don zhugs pa rnams kyi dben pa ste//byang chub sems dpa' drod nyom de ni bdag nyid nyams//de lta bas na byang chub dam pa tshol ba yi//bsam pa 
23b1(sna tshogs): drag ldan mkhas pas nga rgyal nges bcom ste//nad pa'i tshogs kyi gso phyir sman pa bsten pa ltar/ O /g.yel ba med par dge ba'i bshes gnyen brtan par bya//
23b2(sna tshogs): sangs rgyas byang chub mchog zhugs byang chub sems dpa' dag//pha rol phyin dang bcas ste dge ba'i bshes la brten//de dag rjesu ston byed 'di ni sgrub pa'i sa//
23b3(sna tshogs): rgyu rnams gnyis kyis sangs rgyas byang chub myur du rtogs//rgyal ba 'das dang ma byon gang bzhugs pa//kun lam pha rol phyin pa 'di yin gzhan ma yin//pha rol 
23b4(sna tshogs): byang chub mchog la zhugs rnams kyis gsal dang //sgron dang snang dang ston mchog yin zhes bshad//ji ltar shes rab pha rol phyin 'di mtshan nyid stong//chos 'di 
23b5(sna tshogs): thams cad mtshan nyid de dang 'drar shes shing//chos rnams stong zhing mtshan ma med par rab shes nas//de ltar spyod pa bde gshegs shes rab spyod pa yin//sems 
23b6(sna tshogs): can yongs su rtog pas zas la 'dod byed cing//'khor bar chags pa'i yid dang ldan rnams brtag tu 'khor//bdag dang bdag gi chos gnyis yang dag min stong ste/
23b7(sna tshogs): byis pa bdag nyid kyis ni mkha' la 'dud pa bor//dper na dog ba'i 'du shes kyis ni dug langs pa//dug de khong du song ba med kyang rgyal bar 'gyur//de bzhin 
24a1(sna tshogs): @#/ O /byis pa bdag dang bdag gir khas len pa//bdag der 'du shes yang dag mi rtag skye zhing 'chi//ji ltar 'dzin pa de

24a3(sna tshogs): dpa' shes rab pha rol phyin pa rtogs//'dzam bu'i gling na sems can ji snyed ma lus pa//de dag thams cad byang chub mchog tu sems bskyed nas//lo stong bye ba 
24a4(sna tshogs): mang po sbyin pa byin byas la//kun kyang 'gro ba don phyir byang chub rgyur bsngo yang//gang zhig shes rab pha rol phyin (pa) pa la mngon brtson pa//tha na nyi ma gcig gi rjes

24b1(sna tshogs): sems can khams la sten 'dod dang //bdag nyid man ngag mtshan la ban nas bya//mi zhig spyan chad ma rnyed pa yi rin chen mchog//dus gzhan zhig    
24b2(sna tshogs): nas rnyed par 'gyur de dga' ba lus Izhing ma thag tu bag yod du ma ston na ni//ldan pas rin chen mngon 'dod rtag tu sdug bsngal lo//de ltar byang chub mchog

24b4(sna tshogs): bar 'gyur ba bzhin//nyi ma sprin dang bral ba'i 'od zer 'di ba can//mun na I gribs pa'i mun pa thams cad 'joms 'char ba/srin bu me khyer srog chag+sa
24b5(sna tshogs): 'byung po thams cad dang//skar ma'i tshogs dang zla ba'i 'od kun zil gyi gnon//de bzhin byang chub sem+sa dpa' shes rab pha rol phyin//mchog spyod stong dar 
24b6(sna tshogs): pa ni mtshan ma med spyod pa//mkhas pas lta ba'i thib po bcom nas 'gra kun dang//dgra bcom rang+sa rgyal byang chub sems mang zil gyis gnon//dper 
24b7(sna tshogs): na rgyal po'i bu zhig nor gtong don 'ded pa//kun gyi gtso bor gyur te mngon par 'gro bya 'os//'di na da lta na yang sem+sa can mang dga' byed//thabs thobs 
25a1(sna tshogs): gyur te rgyal srid gnas na spos ci dgos//de ltar shes rab spyod mkhas byang chub sems dpa' yang//bdud

25a5(sna tshogs): don lta//de tshe lus sems dag ni mkhas 'gro bya ltar sgyu//nag po'i rtsa lag rnams kyis glags rnyed ga la 'gyur//gang tshe byang chub sems dpa' 
25a6(sna tshogs): 'thab dang brtson gyur cing//phan tshun mi 'thun khros pa'i sems dang ldan gyur pa//de tshe bdud ni mchog tu dga' zhing nyams bder 'gyur//de gnyis rgyal 
25a7(sna tshogs): ba'i ye shes las ni rang 'gyur snyam//de gnyis ring du 'gyur zhing //ba 'ad bar 'gyur//gnyis gzhung rang gi dam bcas nyams par byed par 'gyur//sdang 
25b1(sna tshogs): zhing bzod dang bral la bdud bag la yod na de dag dang rnams pa gsuM dang bcas te don la bar gyur/lung bstan pa thabs byang chub sems dpa' gang zhig gis/
25b2(sna tshogs): lung bstan thob la sems khros pa rnaMs byed na//thub skom phun sems ba ska cog ji skad du/de snyed skal pa bzhi nas go cha bgo bgos so//sangs rgyas bzod pa'i 
25b3(sna tshogs): pha rol phyin pas byang chub dag sems 'di bzang po min snyam pa ngan pa bskyed byed de//so sor 'chags par byed cing phyis kyang sdom byed la//dga' bar mi 
25b4(sna tshogs): byed 'di ni sangs rgyas chos rjes slob//gang zhig slob tshe bslab pa gang yang mkhas mi len//slob pa po dang bslab pa'i chos rnams mi dmigs shing//slab dang 
25b5(sna tshogs): slab pa ma yin 'di gnyis mi rtog pa//de ltar gang slab de ni sangs rgyas chos 'dir slab//(byang chub )sems dpa' gang zhig de ni lha'i slab 'di shes//de ni bzhin yang

26a1(sna tshogs): @#// O //la ni mun pa mi gnas bzhin///sa rab pha rol phyin la bslab par byas rnams kyi //pha rol phyin pa thams cad 
26a2(sna tshogs): 'dir ni 'dus par 'gyur//'jig tshogs lta bar lta ba drug bcu gnyis 'dus ltar//de bzhin pha rol phyin par 'di dag 'dus par gsungs//dper na srog gi dbang po

26a7(sna tshogs): phung po la yang yid rang 'gyur//de phyir de dag rgyal ba'i yon tan thob gyur nas//sdug bsngal zad par bya phyir 'jig rten chos sbyin byed//byang chub sems (dpa')gang 
26b1(sna tshogs): zhig mi rtog chos rnams kun//stong dang mtshan ma man dang spros med yongs zhes pa//don gyi rab gnyis kyis byang chub sel mi byed//rnal 'byor pa de shes rab pha

26b3(sna tshogs): dpa' yang//nam mkha' lta bu yin te nye bar ni spyod pa//skye ba'i dad man sgyu ma'i skyes bu 'di snyam da//skye bo 'di mgu bya snyam mi sems de yang byed//rdzu 'phrul sna tshog+sa

26b7(sna tshogs): shing mkhan bzo bo mkhas pas skyes pa bud med dang//'dra ba'i bzo byas de yang bya ba thams cad byed //de bzhin shes rab spyo mkhas byang chub sems dba' yang/
27a1(sna tshogs): @#//rnam par mi rtog ye shes bya ba thams cad byed//de ltar spyod mkhas rnams la lha tshogs mang po dag/
27a2(sna tshogs): thal mo sbyar ba btud nas phyag kyang 'tshal bar byed//phyog bcu'i 'jig rten khams kyi sangs rgyas ji snyed pa 'ang//yon tan bsngags pa'i phreng ba yongasu brjod par mdzad/
27a3(sna tshogs): gang ga'i klung mnyam zhing na sems can ji snyed pa//de kun yongasu brtag pa zung ste bdud gyur la//ba spu nyag re las kyang de snyed sprul byas kyang//de dag kun gyis     
27a4(sna tshogs): mkhas la bar chad bya mi nus//rgyu rnams bzhi yis byung du ba sems mkhas stobs ldan//bdud bzhis thub par dka' zhing skyod par mi nus te//stong par gnas dang

292b5(sna tshogs): dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /ma bcings pa dang /ma grol ba dang /snang ba med pa dang /mun pa med pa'i rang bzhin du shes par bya'o//de lta bas na byang chub sems dpa'
